- **g-fidelity-critic**: Adversarial G0b plan-fidelity critic. Fires on the first action after a plan-approved dispatch to confirm the action conforms to the approved plan. Use when the orchestrator dispatches g-fidelity-critic 3 times in parallel (asymmetric vote) before the first plan-execution step. &lt;example&gt;Context: G0 returned SOUND last turn; the orchestrator is about to invoke the first task. user: implicit. assistant: "Dispatching g-fidelity-critic ×3 to confirm fidelity to plan." &lt;commentary&gt;G0b is per-action, not per-plan.&lt;/commentary&gt;&lt;/example&gt; ([ElNiak/panther-ivy-plugin](https://github.com/ElNiak/panther-ivy-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/panther-ivy-plugin/agents/g-fidelity-critic.md))
- **g-forge-dev**: Use proactively to run this repo's test suites and g-dev/ gate fixtures and return attested runner output. The execution counterpart to test-writer, which has no execution tool and cannot run anything — g-forge-dev closes that gap for THIS repo's own dogfooded plugin code only. ([onlygian/G-Forge](https://github.com/onlygian/G-Forge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/g-forge-dev.md) · ★4)
- **G-GPT**: Custom agent just to set model to GPT family. ([Crispy13/copilot-directions](https://github.com/Crispy13/copilot-directions/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/G-GPT.agent.md))
- **g-knowledge-critic**: Adversarial G6 knowledge-capture critic. Fires at session-end to identify learnings worth persisting (new patterns, fix strategies, surprising verdicts). Use when the orchestrator dispatches g-knowledge-critic 3 times in parallel before writing learnings to insights.md or feedback memory entries. &lt;example&gt;Context: session is wrapping up; orchestrator about to fire G6. user: implicit (Stop hook context). assistant: "Dispatching g-knowledge-critic ×3 for G6 vote." &lt;commentary&gt;G6 prevents over-capture and under-capture.&lt;/commentary&gt;&lt;/example&gt; ([ElNiak/panther-ivy-plugin](https://github.com/ElNiak/panther-ivy-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/panther-ivy-plugin/agents/g-knowledge-critic.md))
- **G-opus5-high**: **Model: Opus 5. Effort: high.** Reason: the failure mode here is a green result that is actually a blind spot. D2's capture method was wrong three separate ways, two caught by a control and one caught only by an outside reviewer re-deriving the numbers from the pcaps. Wall clock is dominated by hardware, so a faster model buys nothing and costs the careful reading §14 now demands. - **g2g-deducer**: Deduction agent for Grand-to-Ground research cycles and Theory Forge rounds. Cycle mode — performs a falsification-surface inventory, then derives consequences strictly inside a given axiom set and declared assumptions, marks all guesses and all agent-added shapes, ends with a pairwise collision self-check, and self-assesses weak steps. - **g2g-defender**: Independent defender for the G2G tribunal. Fresh-context agent that answers every objection with three-value verdicts (defended / partly defended / conceded), recomputes every computable allegation directly from the programme's data files instead of arguing it, proposes replacement language for killed sentences, and nominates reusable defense pieces. Optional at standard severity, mandatory at submission severity, and called at any tier before a claim is weakened on an objection that shows no fault. In the theory tribunal (TT, Forge milestones) there is no original author, so only this agent… ([HongtaoMa1/grand-to-ground](https://github.com/HongtaoMa1/grand-to-ground/blob/HEAD/agents/g2g-defender.md) · ★3)
- **g2g-evidence-critic**: Evidence critic for the G2G tribunal. Attacks conflicts with data, history, and documented facts using live web search; runs carrier checks on institutional anchors and must quote the source verbatim with its position; verifies every marked guess; holds the sole privilege of attacking the protected hard core (attacks are logged, not adjudicated). - **g2g-logic-critic**: Logic critic for the G2G tribunal. Cycle and plan tribunals — attacks internal validity only (gaps, circularity, smuggled assumptions, equivocation, ratio/total confusion, self-cancelled bets, drift from declared assumptions, bare numbers used as criteria, citations without the verbatim original). Theory tribunal (TT, Forge milestones) — checks cross-node consistency and relevance/value only; never re-verifies a derivation that has passed the verification gate. Respects the exemption rule (declared assumptions and ratified axioms are not litigated) and standing rulings. - **g2g-novelty-critic**: Novelty critic for the G2G tribunal. Checks every conclusion against existing literature with live search on two axes (method family and application domain) — has this been done, and better? Grades the deduction's pre-coded novelty claims (STANDS / SAME), names nearest prior work with similarity ratings, and states how each claim differs from it. In the theory tribunal (TT, Forge milestones) it asks "has anyone derived this algebra before — and from weaker axioms?" for the milestone subgraph's theorems. - **g2g-verifier**: The verification gate of the Grand-to-Ground Theory Forge. - **g5-문안제작**: 세그먼트 대표 1곳의 제안 메일 문안을 만들거나(모드 M), 그 대표 문안의 틀을 같은 세그먼트 소속 기업으로 확장 생성하거나(모드 E), 번역 검증이 지목한 불일치 지점만 받아 일본어를 다시 만든다(모드 R). 한국어 발신 의도 전문과 일본어 발신 문안(제목·본문)을 짝으로 내고 법정 표시 의무 4종을 양쪽에 고정 포함한다. 제안 메일 제작·발송(G5) Workflow의 **문안 단위**이며 `g5-제안메일제작발송` 스킬이 호출한다. 번역 검증은 하지 않는다 — 자기 번역을 자기가 검증하면 검증이 아니므로 별도 단위(`g5-번역검증`)가 맡는다. 파일을 쓰지 않고 발송하지 않는다. ([lgosoup/keunsamchon-agent-plan](https://github.com/lgosoup/keunsamchon-agent-plan/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/g5-%EB%AC%B8%EC%95%88%EC%A0%9C%EC%9E%91.md))
- **g5-번역검증**: 한국어 발신 의도 원문과 일본어 발신 문안 **둘만** 받아 번역 검증 3층(의미 보존·고유명사와 수치·어조와 방향)을 대조하고 층별 일치/불일치와 불일치 지점을 낸다. 제안 메일 제작·발송(G5) Workflow의 **검증 단위 V**이며 `g5-제안메일제작발송` 스킬이 호출한다. 방향이 G7과 반대다 — 원문이 한국어이고 번역이 일본어이며, 판정 문장도 다른 행을 쓴다. 번역을 고치지도 파일을 쓰지도 않는다. ([lgosoup/keunsamchon-agent-plan](https://github.com/lgosoup/keunsamchon-agent-plan/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/g5-%EB%B2%88%EC%97%AD%EA%B2%80%EC%A6%9D.md))
- **g5-승인확인**: 발송대기 화면 파일 1개를 받아 승인란(승인/거부 값·승인자·승인일시)만 읽고, 건마다 승인/거부/미승인 판정을 반환한다. 제안 메일 제작·발송(G5) Workflow의 **승인 판독 단위**이며 `g5-제안메일제작발송` 스킬 발송 모드가 호출한다. 발송 권한이 없고 파일을 쓰지도 고치지도 않는다 — 판독하는 자리와 발송 권한을 쥔 자리가 같으면 게이트가 아니다. ([lgosoup/keunsamchon-agent-plan](https://github.com/lgosoup/keunsamchon-agent-plan/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/g5-%EC%8A%B9%EC%9D%B8%ED%99%95%EC%9D%B8.md))

- **ga4-analyst**: Specialist for Google Analytics 4: sessions, users, page views, traffic sources/channels, landing-page behavior, events, conversions, and funnels. Use whenever a question involves website behavior, on-site conversions, or attribution of traffic to outcomes. ([faiyazmn/ad-analytics](https://github.com/faiyazmn/ad-analytics/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ga4-analyst.md))
- **ga4-attribution**: Read GA4 attribution settings and session-scoped channel performance. Distinguish session acquisition from attributed key-event credit. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-attribution.md))
- **ga4-audience-builder**: GA4 audience builder. Proposes Audience definitions based on observed funnel drop-off and ecomm event volumes, then creates them via the Admin API after user confirmation. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-audience-builder.md))
- **ga4-context**: GA4 property profiler. Reads the property's web stream, fetches the live site, and produces a human-readable summary of what the property is (vertical, platform, framework, page-type inventory, language) so subsequent agents can ground their findings. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-context.md))
- **ga4-conversions**: Inspect key-event configuration and count/value reports without conflating event frequency with session conversion rate. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-conversions.md))
- **ga4-events**: Inspect event presence and the limited parameter proxies implemented by ga4_events.py. Keep unsupported checks and hypotheses explicit. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-events.md))
- **ga4-events-editor**: GA4 event editor. Proposes EventEditRule and EventCreateRule definitions to fix taxonomy issues found by ga4-events, then writes them via the Admin API after user confirmation. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-events-editor.md))
- **ga4-funnel**: GA4 funnel analyst. Builds an N-step funnel from any user-supplied event list, computes step conversion and drop-off rates, identifies the leakiest step, and supports breakdown by a single dimension. Works for purchase funnels, lead funnels, signup flows, content-engagement funnels - anything expressible as an ordered list of GA4 events. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-funnel.md))
- **ga4-property**: Audit available GA4 property, stream, retention, custom-definition and link settings. Label unavailable settings as manual checks. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-property.md))
- **ga4-quality**: Inspect sampling and response metadata, source coverage and diagnostic trends. Mark incomplete checks and avoid unsupported causal claims. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-quality.md))
- **ga4-reader**: GA4(Bideasy Web 속성) 트래픽 스냅샷 수집. "GA4 확인해줘", "이번 달 트래픽", "베이스라인 갱신" 요청 시 사용. 읽기 전용. ([hosicompany/Bideasy](https://github.com/hosicompany/Bideasy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ga4-reader.md))

- **game-jam**: A partir de un tema creativo, diseña un juego retro completo para Arcade Vault y genera 3 specs en specs/game-jam/[slug]/ (concepto, diseño técnico, integración). Valida slugs contra references/game-suggestions-todo.md y lib/data.ts, registra el juego como PROPUESTO en la memoria compartida con game-planner. Úsalo cuando el usuario diga "hagamos un game jam", "diseñá un juego con el tema X", "game-jam &lt;tema&gt;", o similar. No pregunta nada, no escribe código ni SQL aplicado, y deja todo en Borrador. Úsalo cuando el usuario pida una game jam, tres juegos de golpe, o conceptos de juego sobre un tema.
